### Current file ownership / conflicts

#### Source-traced runtime integration gap · 2026-09-05

Status: **implementation queued; in-app/native acceptance blocked on the missing adapters**. Normal `src/main.jsx` mounting supplies no runtime capability receipts. MCP preference/eligibility controls do not establish a connection; the standalone stdio verifier is not a UI event producer. Vite has only development collision/folder/component middleware, while native commands cover runtime/picker/census/collision—not MCP or transaction recovery. The existing transaction store can inspect pending records, but there is no authority-owned project-to-store catalog or Operations transport. No live connection, recovery action or completion is claimed by this audit.

- [ ] T82 / C031, C027, C034, C032: fixed, bounded native MCP service; exact native registration plus project/generation binding; real handshake and project metadata read; trusted observation provenance; connection failure, expiry and revocation handling. Keep browser-only folders explicitly unavailable without native registration.
- [ ] T80 / C035, C020: mount the runtime provider; ingest bounded redacted outcomes with request IDs and scope captured at request start; expose Check connection while disconnected. Do not attribute late failures to a newly selected project or treat local diagnostic storage/imported JSON as authority.
- [ ] T85 / C018, C027, C020: authority-owned registered-repository transaction-store discovery, project-filtered read-only pending summaries, and visible retained-work/reconciliation state. Refuse arbitrary client store paths; never auto-delete or promote from inspection.
- [ ] Acceptance: packaged/native round trip, missing/crashed service, timeout/cancellation, project-switch races, wrong/stale/revoked observations, incomplete/corrupt journals, reload discovery and browser-unavailable behavior. UI changes require before/after and interaction proof; fixture or standalone smoke receipts cannot close these items.

Detailed contract and dependency order: `docs/architecture/maps/06-private-exchange-adapters.md`; UI contract: `docs/architecture/maps/08-ui-shell-workspaces.md`. No endpoints, configuration, native permissions or user-repository changes were made during the audit.

## ROADMAP-20260926-01 — native session lifetime integration (IN PROGRESS)
